**[00:18:50]** We also know that from looking at empirical evidence.

**[00:18:52]** The universe is created.

**[00:18:54]** So you have to have an uncaused cause.

**[00:18:56]** And that means that whatever caused this is itself causeless.

**[00:19:03]** The creator himself is self-existent.

**[00:19:07]** And that's a profound understanding because that means that his existence is vastly different from ours.

**[00:19:13]** He's the source of life.

**[00:19:15]** He upholds the universe, all the things that the Bible tells you.

**[00:19:17]** But again, you don't need a Bible to infer these things from creation.

**[00:19:21]** We also know from.

**[00:19:22]** Animals and humans.

**[00:19:23]** Everything is personal.

**[00:19:25]** Even little bugs and things that we don't attribute too much intelligence to, like maybe a little shark or a fish or something.

**[00:19:33]** They have their own personalities.

**[00:19:35]** Even things that we think are stupid or much stupider than us.

**[00:19:39]** They have their own personalities.

**[00:19:40]** They only have their own individual sense of things, which tells you that the creator is a person.

**[00:19:49]** In other words, the creator is not some mechanism.
